# Warm-path relationship graph — auto-mined 2026-08-13

> Generated by mining Jefferson's own communications. Sources: (1) Attio CRM people
> records with interaction data (8,776 people pulled; 2,868 with real interactions after
> junk filtering); (2) SENT-thread sweeps of the four Superhuman mailboxes —
> jefferson.stovall@gmail.com (500 threads, back to 2025-01-27),
> jeffersons@adaptiverseapp.com (500, back to 2025-08-25),
> jeffersons@captaintomorrow.io (500, back to 2025-07-03),
> jefferson@reacheveryvoice.org (7 threads = complete mailbox); (3) the curated 18-name
> list in `background/network.md` (kept there as seeds, not duplicated here).
>
> Tiers: **WARM** = confirmed two-way correspondence (both directions in a sent thread)
> or a calendar meeting within the last 18 months (since 2025-02-13). **DORMANT** = real
> two-way correspondence or meetings, but older. **THIN** = one-way, campaign/list mail,
> or Attio email interactions whose direction can't be confirmed. Newsletters, no-reply
> senders, role accounts (info@/support@/etc.), vendor notification domains, and
> meeting-bot addresses were excluded.
>
> Watchlist? = the person's org matches one of the 88 orgs in `../watchlist.md`
> (tier letter: org). Coverage caveats: the adv mailbox sweep only reaches back 12
> months and gmail 18.5 months (500-thread cap per account), so older correspondence
> is represented only through Attio, where mail direction is unknown — some genuinely
> warm pre-2025 relationships will sit in THIN. Names for people not in Attio are
> derived from the email address.
>
> Everything local. Nothing was sent; no one was contacted.
